The Table of the Structure Constants for the Complex Simple Lie Algebra of Type F 4 F_{4} and its Application to the Calculation of Commutators in the Chevalley Group of Type F 4 F_{4} over Fields and Rings
Sergey G. Kolesnikov, Anna I. Polovinkina

Abstract
. This work is the first in a series of papers devoted to constructing tables of structure constants for the complex simple Lie algebras and to finding an explicit form of Chevalley commutator formulas. The work consists of three parts. In the first part, expressions are found for the structure constants of the complex simple Lie algebra of type F 4 F_{4} in the form of functions of structure constants corresponding to extraspecial pairs of roots. As a consequence, all Chevalley commutator formulas [ x r ​ ( u ) , x s ​ ( y ) ] [x_{r}(u),x_{s}(y)] are calculated when the sum r + s r+s is a root. Further, in the second part, tables of structure constants and Chevalley commutator formulas are given in the special case when all constants corresponding to extraspecial pairs are equal to one. Finally, in the third part, directed and weighted graphs associated with root systems are constructed. It is shown that the elements of the exponent of the adjacency matrices of directed graphs are the numbers P r ​ s P_{rs} , where P r ​ s P_{rs} is the number of representations of the root r r in the form of a sum of the root s s and fundamental roots such that any initial segment of the sum is a root. It is also shown that the elements of an exponent of the weight matrix of a weighted graph are the values of sums arising when calculating complex commutators in Chevalley groups. Keywords: Structure constant of the complex simple Lie algebra, root system, Chevalley commutator formulas.



1.
The Table of the Structure Constants

1.1.
Information about the Root System of Type
F_{4}

Following
[
1
]
, we choose the orthonormal basis
e_{1},e_{2},e_{3},e_{4}
in the Euclidean space
V=\mathbb{R}^{4}
with the scalar product
(\,,\,).
Then the following set of vectors:

\pm e_{i}\ (1\leqslant i\leqslant 4),\quad\pm e_{i}\pm e_{j}\ (1\leqslant i\leqslant 4),\quad(\pm e_{1}\pm e_{2}\pm e_{3}\pm e_{4})/2

forms the reduced indecomposable root system of type
F_{4}.

The following subsets

\{a=e_{2}-e_{1},\quad b=e_{3}-e_{2},\quad c=e_{4},\quad d=(e_{1}-e_{2}-e_{3}-e_{4})/2\}

and

\{e_{i}\ (1\leqslant i\leqslant 4),\quad e_{i}\pm e_{j}\ (1\leqslant i\leqslant 4),\quad(e_{1}\pm e_{2}\pm e_{3}\pm e_{4})/2\}

form, respectively, the system of fundamental roots
\Pi(F_{4})
and the system of positive roots

F_{4}^{+}
in
F_{4}.
Next, for convenience, we represent the positive root

r=\alpha a+\beta b+\gamma c+\delta d

by the quadruple of numbers
\alpha\beta\gamma\delta.
The sum
\alpha+\beta+\gamma+\delta

is called the height of the root
r
and is denoted by
{\rm ht}(r).

We define the order
\prec
on
V
, consistent with the choice of the fundamental root system, as follows.
For arbitrary vectors
x,y\in V
we assume

x=\tau_{1}a+\tau_{2}b+\tau_{3}c+\tau_{4}d\prec y=\rho_{1}a+\rho_{2}b+\rho_{3}c+\rho_{4}d

if and only if the first coefficient in the expansion of the difference

y-x=(\rho_{1}-\tau_{1})a+(\rho_{2}-\tau_{2})b+(\rho_{3}-\tau_{3})c+(\rho_{4}-\tau_{4})d

is positive. With respect to the order
\prec
the positive roots of the root system
F_{4}
are ordered in the following way:

0001\prec 0010\prec 0011\prec 0100\prec 0110\prec 0111\prec 0120\prec 0121\prec 0122\prec 1000\prec 1100\prec

1110\prec 1111\prec 1120\prec 1121\prec 1122\prec 1220\prec 1221\prec 1222\prec 1231\prec 1232\prec 1242\prec

1342\prec 2342.

The following table shows the correspondence between the number of a positive root in the ordered list and its quadruple representation.
The table also shows root height and length.

(028)

Table 4, continue.

Proof.

According to
[
2
, Theorem 4.1.2]
the structure constants of a simple Lie algebra of type
\Phi
over

\mathbb{C}
satisfy the following relations:

(i)
\displaystyle{N_{s,r}=-N_{r,s},\ r,s\in\Phi;}

(ii)
\displaystyle{\frac{N_{r_{1},r_{2}}}{(r_{3},r_{3})}=\frac{N_{r_{2},r_{3}}}{(r_{1},r_{1})}=\frac{N_{r_{3},r_{1}}}{(r_{2},r_{2})},}

if
\displaystyle{r_{1},r_{2},r_{3}\in\Phi}
satisfy

\displaystyle{r_{1}+r_{2}+r_{3}=0;}

(iii)
\displaystyle{N_{r,s}N_{-r,-s}=-(p+1)^{2},}

if
r,s,r+s\in\Phi;

(iv)

\displaystyle{\frac{N_{r_{1},r_{2}}N_{r_{3},r_{4}}}{(r_{1}+r_{2},r_{1}+r_{2})}+\frac{N_{r_{2},r_{3}}N_{r_{1},r_{4}}}{(r_{2}+r_{3},r_{2}+r_{3})}+\frac{N_{r_{3},r_{1}}N_{r_{2},r_{4}}}{(r_{3}+r_{1},r_{3}+r_{1})}=0,}

if
\displaystyle{r_{1},r_{2},r_{3},r_{4}\in\Phi}
satisfy

\displaystyle{r_{1}+r_{2}+r_{3}+r_{4}=0}
and if no pair opposite.

001. We have
d+(c)+(-c-d)=0,
therefore (see Table 2)

[formula]

N_{d,c}=N_{c,-c-d}=N_{-c-d,d}=\epsilon_{1}.

001. We have
d+(c)+(-c-d)=0,
therefore (see Table 2)

[formula]

N_{d,c}=N_{c,-c-d}=N_{-c-d,d}=\epsilon_{1}.

002. We have
c+(b)+(-b-c)=0,
therefore (see Table 2)

[formula]

\frac{N_{c,b}}{1}=\frac{N_{b,-b-c}}{1}=\frac{N_{-b-c,c}}{2}=\delta_{1}.

003. We have
d+(b+c)+(-b-c-d)=0,
therefore (see Table 2)

[formula]

N_{d,b+c}=N_{b+c,-b-c-d}=N_{-b-c-d,d}=\epsilon_{2}.

004. We have
c+(b+c)+(-b-2c)=0,
therefore (see Table 2)

[formula]

\frac{N_{c,b+c}}{2}=\frac{N_{b+c,-b-2c}}{1}=\frac{N_{-b-2c,c}}{1}=\delta_{2}.

005. We have
d+(b+2c)+(-b-2c-d)=0,
therefore (see Table 2)

[formula]

\frac{N_{d,b+2c}}{1}=\frac{N_{b+2c,-b-2c-d}}{1}=\frac{N_{-b-2c-d,d}}{2}=\epsilon_{3}.

006. We have
d+(b+2c+d)+(-b-2c-2d)=0,
therefore (see Table 2)

[formula]

\frac{N_{d,b+2c+d}}{2}=\frac{N_{b+2c+d,-b-2c-2d}}{1}=\frac{N_{-b-2c-2d,d}}{1}=\epsilon_{4}.

007. We have
d+(c)+b+(-b-c-d)=0
and
b+d\notin\ F_{4},
means

S=\frac{N_{d,c}N_{b,-b-c-d}}{1}+\frac{N_{c,b}N_{d,-b-c-d}}{1}=0.

Since

N_{d,c}N_{b,-b-c-d}=|\text{Table 2}|=\epsilon_{1}N_{b,-b-c-d},

N_{c,b}N_{d,-b-c-d}=|\text{Table 2 and formula (003)}|=\delta_{1}(-\epsilon_{2})=-\epsilon_{2}\delta_{1},

we have

S=\epsilon_{1}N_{b,-b-c-d}-\epsilon_{2}\delta_{1}=0

and therefore

[formula]

N_{b,-b-c-d}=\epsilon_{12}\delta_{1}.

008. We have
(c+d)+b+(-b-c-d)=0,
therefore (see formula (007))

[formula]

\frac{N_{c+d,b}}{1}=\frac{N_{b,-b-c-d}}{1}=\frac{N_{-b-c-d,c+d}}{2}=\epsilon_{12}\delta_{1}.

009. We have
(c+d)+(b+c)+(-b-2c)+(-d)=0
and
(-b-2c)+(c+d)\notin\ F_{4},
means

S=\frac{N_{c+d,b+c}N_{-b-2c,-d}}{1}+\frac{N_{b+c,-b-2c}N_{c+d,-d}}{1}=0.

Since

N_{c+d,b+c}N_{-b-2c,-d}=|\text{Table 2}|=\epsilon_{3}{N_{c+d,b+c}},

N_{b+c,-b-2c}N_{c+d,-d}=|\text{Formulas (004) and (001)}|=\delta_{2}(-\epsilon_{1})=-\epsilon_{1}\delta_{2},

we have

S=\epsilon_{3}N_{c+d,b+c}-\epsilon_{1}\delta_{2}=0

and therefore

[formula]

N_{c+d,b+c}=\epsilon_{13}\delta_{2}.

010. We have
(c+d)+(b+c)+(-b-2c-d)=0,
therefore (see formula (009))

[formula]

N_{c+d,b+c}=N_{b+c,-b-2c-d}=N_{-b-2c-d,c+d}=\epsilon_{13}\delta_{2}.

011. We have
c+(b+c+d)+(-b-c)+(-c-d)=0,
means

S=\frac{N_{c,b+c+d}N_{-b-c,-c-d}}{1}+\frac{N_{b+c+d,-b-c}N_{c,-c-d}}{1}+\frac{N_{-b-c,c}N_{b+c+d,-c-d}}{2}=0.

Since

N_{c,b+c+d}N_{-b-c,-c-d}=|\text{Formula (009)}|=\epsilon_{13}\delta_{2}N_{c,b+c+d},

N_{b+c+d,-b-c}N_{c,-c-d}=|\text{Formulas (003) and (001)}|=\epsilon_{2}(\epsilon_{1})=\epsilon_{12},

N_{-b-c,c}N_{b+c+d,-c-d}=|\text{Formulas (002) and (008)}|=2\delta_{1}(-2\epsilon_{12}\delta_{1})=-4\epsilon_{12},

we have

S=\epsilon_{13}\delta_{2}N_{c,b+c+d}+\epsilon_{12}-\frac{4\epsilon_{12}}{2}=\epsilon_{13}\delta_{2}N_{c,b+c+d}-\epsilon_{12}=0,

and therefore

[formula]

N_{c,b+c+d}=\epsilon_{23}\delta_{2}.

012. We have
c+(b+c+d)+(-b-2c-d)=0,
therefore (see formula (011))

[formula]

N_{c,b+c+d}=N_{b+c+d,-b-2c-d}=N_{-b-2c-d,c}=\epsilon_{23}\delta_{2}.

013. We have
(c+d)+(b+c+d)+(-b-2c-d)+(-d)=0,
means

S=\frac{N_{c+d,b+c+d}N_{-b-2c-d,-d}}{2}+\frac{N_{b+c+d,-b-2c-d}N_{c+d,-d}}{1}+\frac{N_{-b-2c-d,c+d}N_{b+c+d,-d}}{1}=0.

Since

N_{c+d,b+c+d}N_{-b-2c-d,-d}=|\text{Table 2}|=2\epsilon_{4}N_{c+d,b+c+d},

N_{b+c+d,-b-2c-d}N_{c+d,-d}=|\text{Formulas (012) and (001)}|=\epsilon_{23}\delta_{2}(-\epsilon_{1})=-\epsilon_{123}\delta_{2},

N_{-b-2c-d,c+d}N_{b+c+d,-d}=|\text{Formulas (010) and (003)}|=\epsilon_{13}\delta_{2}(-\epsilon_{2})=-\epsilon_{123}\delta_{2},

we have

S=\frac{2\epsilon_{4}N_{c+d,b+c+d}}{2}-\epsilon_{123}\delta_{2}-\epsilon_{123}\delta_{2}=0

and therefore

[formula]

N_{c+d,b+c+d}=2\epsilon_{1234}\delta_{2}.

014. We have
(c+d)+(b+c+d)+(-b-2c-2d)=0,
therefore (see formula (013))

[formula]

\frac{N_{c+d,b+c+d}}{2}=\frac{N_{b+c+d,-b-2c-2d}}{1}=\frac{N_{-b-2c-2d,c+d}}{1}=\epsilon_{1234}\delta_{2}.

015. We have
d+(a+b+c)+(-a-b-c-d)=0,
therefore (see Table 2)

[formula]

N_{d,a+b+c}=N_{a+b+c,-a-b-c-d}=N_{-a-b-c-d,d}=\epsilon_{5}.

016. We have
d+(a+b+2c)+(-a-b-2c-d)=0,
therefore (see Table 2)

[formula]

\frac{N_{d,a+b+2c}}{1}=\frac{N_{a+b+2c,-a-b-2c-d}}{1}=\frac{N_{-a-b-2c-d,d}}{2}=\epsilon_{6}.

017. We have
d+(a+b+2c+d)+(-a-b-2c-2d)=0,
therefore (see Table 2)

[formula]

\frac{N_{d,a+b+2c+d}}{2}=\frac{N_{a+b+2c+d,-a-b-2c-2d}}{1}=\frac{N_{-a-b-2c-2d,d}}{1}=\epsilon_{7}.

018. We have
d+(a+2b+2c)+(-a-2b-2c-d)=0,
therefore (see Table 2)

[formula]

\frac{N_{d,a+2b+2c}}{1}=\frac{N_{a+2b+2c,-a-2b-2c-d}}{1}=\frac{N_{-a-2b-2c-d,d}}{2}=\epsilon_{8}.

019. We have
d+(a+2b+2c+d)+(-a-2b-2c-2d)=0,
therefore (see Table 2)

[formula]

\frac{N_{d,a+2b+2c+d}}{2}=\frac{N_{a+2b+2c+d,-a-2b-2c-2d}}{1}=\frac{N_{-a-2b-2c-2d,d}}{1}=\epsilon_{9}.

020. We have
d+(a+2b+3c+d)+(-a-2b-3c-2d)=0,
therefore (see Table 2)

[formula]

N_{d,a+2b+3c+d}=N_{a+2b+3c+d,-a-2b-3c-2d}=N_{-a-2b-3c-2d,d}=\epsilon_{0}.

021. We have
c+(a+b)+(-a-b-c)=0,
therefore (see Table 2)

[formula]

\frac{N_{c,a+b}}{1}=\frac{N_{a+b,-a-b-c}}{1}=\frac{N_{-a-b-c,c}}{2}=\delta_{3}.

022. We have
c+(a+b+c)+(-a-b-2c)=0,
therefore (see Table 2)

[formula]

\frac{N_{c,a+b+c}}{2}=\frac{N_{a+b+c,-a-b-2c}}{1}=\frac{N_{-a-b-2c,c}}{1}=\delta_{4}.

023. We have
c+(a+2b+2c+d)+(-a-2b-3c-d)=0,
therefore (see Table 2)

[formula]

N_{c,a+2b+2c+d}=N_{a+2b+2c+d,-a-2b-3c-d}=N_{-a-2b-3c-d,c}=\delta_{5}.

024. We have
c+(a+2b+3c+2d)+(-a-2b-4c-2d)=0,
therefore (see Table 2)

[formula]

\frac{N_{c,a+2b+3c+2d}}{2}=\frac{N_{a+2b+3c+2d,-a-2b-4c-2d}}{1}=\frac{N_{-a-2b-4c-2d,c}}{1}=\delta_{6}.

025. We have
b+(a)+(-a-b)=0,
therefore (see Table 2)

[formula]

N_{b,a}=N_{a,-a-b}=N_{-a-b,b}=\gamma_{1}.

026. We have
b+(a+b+2c)+(-a-2b-2c)=0,
therefore (see Table 2)

[formula]

N_{b,a+b+2c}=N_{a+b+2c,-a-2b-2c}=N_{-a-2b-2c,b}=\gamma_{2}.

027. We have
b+(a+2b+4c+2d)+(-a-3b-4c-2d)=0,
therefore (see Table 2)

[formula]

N_{b,a+2b+4c+2d}=N_{a+2b+4c+2d,-a-3b-4c-2d}=N_{-a-3b-4c-2d,b}=\gamma_{3}.

028. We have
a+(a+3b+4c+2d)+(-2a-3b-4c-2d)=0,
therefore (see Table 2)

[formula]

N_{a,a+3b+4c+2d}=N_{a+3b+4c+2d,-2a-3b-4c-2d}=N_{-2a-3b-4c-2d,a}=\alpha_{1}.

029. We have
c+(d)+(a+b)+(-a-b-c-d)=0
and
d+(a+b)\notin\ F_{4},
means

S=\frac{N_{c,d}N_{a+b,-a-b-c-d}}{1}+\frac{N_{a+b,c}N_{d,-a-b-c-d}}{1}=0.

Since

N_{c,d}N_{a+b,-a-b-c-d}=|\text{Table 2}|=-\epsilon_{1}N_{a+b,-a-b-c-d},

N_{a+b,c}N_{d,-a-b-c-d}=|\text{Table 2 and formula (015)}|=-\delta_{3}(-\epsilon_{5})=\epsilon_{5}\delta_{3},

we have

S=-\epsilon_{1}N_{a+b,-a-b-c-d}+\epsilon_{5}\delta_{3}=0,

and therefore

[formula]

N_{a+b,-a-b-c-d}=\epsilon_{15}\delta_{3}.

030. We have
(a+b)+(-a-b-c-d)+(c+d)=0,
therefore (see formula (029))

[formula]

\frac{N_{a+b,-a-b-c-d}}{1}=\frac{N_{-a-b-c-d,c+d}}{2}=\frac{N_{c+d,a+b}}{1}=\epsilon_{15}\delta_{3}.

031. We have
(c+d)+(a+b+c)+(-d)+(-a-b-2c)=0
and
(a+b+c)+(-d)\notin\ F_{4},
means

S=\frac{N_{c+d,a+b+c}N_{-d,-a-b-2c}}{1}+\frac{N_{-d,c+d}N_{a+b+c,-a-b-2c}}{1}=0.

Since

N_{c+d,a+b+c}N_{-d,-a-b-2c}=|\text{Table 2}|=-\epsilon_{6}N_{c+d,a+b+c},

N_{-d,c+d}N_{a+b+c,-a-b-2c}=|\text{Formulas (001) and (022)}|=\epsilon_{1}\delta_{4},

we have

S=-\epsilon_{6}N_{c+d,a+b+c}+\epsilon_{1}\delta_{4}=0,

and therefore

[formula]

N_{c+d,a+b+c}=\epsilon_{16}\delta_{4}.

032. We have
(c+d)+(a+b+c)+(-a-b-2c-d)=0,
therefore (see formula (031))

[formula]

N_{c+d,a+b+c}=N_{a+b+c,-a-b-2c-d}=N_{-a-b-2c-d,c+d}=\epsilon_{16}\delta_{4}.

033. We have
c+(a+b+c+d)+(-d)+(-a-b-2c)=0
and
(-d)+c\notin\ F_{4},
means

S=\frac{N_{c,a+b+c+d}N_{-d,-a-b-2c}}{1}+\frac{N_{a+b+c+d,-d}N_{c,-a-b-2c}}{1}=0.

Since

N_{c,a+b+c+d}N_{-d,-a-b-2c}=|\text{Table 2}|=-\epsilon_{6}N_{c,a+b+c+d},

N_{a+b+c+d,-d}N_{c,-a-b-2c}=|\text{Formulas (015) and (022)}|=-\epsilon_{5}(-\delta_{4})=\epsilon_{5}\delta_{4},

we have

S=-\epsilon_{6}N_{c,a+b+c+d}+\epsilon_{5}\delta_{4}=0,

and therefore

[formula]

N_{c,a+b+c+d}=\epsilon_{56}\delta_{4}.

034. We have
c+(a+b+c+d)+(-a-b-2c-d)=0,
therefore (see formula (033))

[formula]

N_{c,a+b+c+d}=N_{a+b+c+d,-a-b-2c-d}=N_{-a-b-2c-d,c}=\epsilon_{56}\delta_{4}.

035. We have
(c+d)+(a+2b+2c)+(-c)+(-a-2b-2c-d)=0
and
(a+2b+2c)+(-c)\notin\ F_{4},
means

S=\frac{N_{c+d,a+2b+2c}N_{-c,-a-2b-2c-d}}{1}+\frac{N_{-c,c+d}N_{a+2b+2c,-a-2b-2c-d}}{1}=0.

Since

N_{c+d,a+2b+2c}N_{-c,-a-2b-2c-d}=|\text{Table 2}|=-\delta_{5}N_{c+d,a+2b+2c},

N_{-c,c+d}N_{a+2b+2c,-a-2b-2c-d}=|\text{Formulas (001) and (018)}|=-\epsilon_{1}(\epsilon_{8})=-\epsilon_{18},

we have

S=-\delta_{5}N_{c+d,a+2b+2c}-\epsilon_{18}=0

and therefore

[formula]

N_{c+d,a+2b+2c}=-\epsilon_{18}\delta_{5}.

036. We have
(c+d)+(a+2b+2c)+(-a-2b-3c-d)=0,
therefore (see formula (035))

[formula]

\frac{N_{c+d,a+2b+2c}}{1}=\frac{N_{a+2b+2c,-a-2b-3c-d}}{1}=\frac{N_{-a-2b-3c-d,c+d}}{2}=-\epsilon_{18}\delta_{5}.

037. We have
(c+d)+(a+2b+2c+d)+(-d)+(-a-2b-3c-d)=0,
means

S=\frac{N_{c+d,a+2b+2c+d}N_{-d,-a-2b-3c-d}}{1}+\frac{N_{a+2b+2c+d,-d}N_{c+d,-a-2b-3c-d}}{2}+

+\frac{N_{-d,c+d}N_{a+2b+2c+d,-a-2b-3c-d}}{1}=0.

Since

N_{c+d,a+2b+2c+d}N_{-d,-a-2b-3c-d}=|\text{Table 2}|=-\epsilon_{0}N_{c+d,a+2b+2c+d},

N_{a+2b+2c+d,-d}N_{c+d,-a-2b-3c-d}=|\text{Formulas (018) and (036)}|=-2\epsilon_{8}(2\epsilon_{18}\delta_{5})=-4\epsilon_{1}\delta_{5},

N_{-d,c+d}N_{a+2b+2c+d,-a-2b-3c-d}=|\text{Formulas (001) and (023)}|=\epsilon_{1}(\delta_{5})=\epsilon_{1}\delta_{5},

we have

S=-\epsilon_{0}N_{c+d,a+2b+2c+d}-\frac{4\epsilon_{1}\delta_{5}}{2}+\epsilon_{1}\delta_{5}=0

and therefore

[formula]

N_{c+d,a+2b+2c+d}=-\epsilon_{10}\delta_{5}.

038. We have
(c+d)+(a+2b+2c+d)+(-a-2b-3c-2d)=0,
therefore (see formula (037))

[formula]

N_{c+d,a+2b+2c+d}=N_{a+2b+2c+d,-a-2b-3c-2d}=N_{-a-2b-3c-2d,c+d}=-\epsilon_{10}\delta_{5}.

039. We have
(c+d)+(a+2b+3c+d)+(-c)+(-a-2b-3c-2d)=0,
means

S=\frac{N_{c+d,a+2b+3c+d}N_{-c,-a-2b-3c-2d}}{2}+\frac{N_{a+2b+3c+d,-c}N_{c+d,-a-2b-3c-2d}}{1}+

+\frac{N_{-c,c+d}N_{a+2b+3c+d,-a-2b-3c-2d}}{1}=0,

Since

N_{c+d,a+2b+3c+d}N_{-c,-a-2b-3c-2d}=|\text{Table 2}|=-2\delta_{6}N_{c+d,a+2b+3c+d},

N_{a+2b+3c+d,-c}N_{c+d,-a-2b-3c-2d}=|\text{Formulas (023) and (038)}|=-\delta_{5}(\epsilon_{10}\delta_{5})=-\epsilon_{10},

N_{-c,c+d}N_{a+2b+3c+d,-a-2b-3c-2d}=|\text{Formulas (001) and (020)}|=-\epsilon_{1}(\epsilon_{0})=-\epsilon_{10},

we have

S=-\frac{2\delta_{6}N_{c+d,a+2b+3c+d}}{2}-\epsilon_{10}-\epsilon_{10}=0

and therefore

[formula]

N_{c+d,a+2b+3c+d}=-2\epsilon_{10}\delta_{6}.

040. We have
(c+d)+(a+2b+3c+d)+(-a-2b-4c-2d)=0,
therefore (see formula (039))

[formula]

\frac{N_{c+d,a+2b+3c+d}}{2}=\frac{N_{a+2b+3c+d,-a-2b-4c-2d}}{1}=\frac{N_{-a-2b-4c-2d,c+d}}{1}=-\epsilon_{10}\delta_{6}.

41. We have
(b+c)+a+(-c)+(-a-b)=0
and
a+(-c)\notin F_{4},
means

S=\frac{N_{b+c,a}N_{-c,-a-b}}{1}+\frac{N_{-c,b+c}N_{a,-a-b}}{2}=0.

Since

N_{b+c,a}N_{-c,-a-b}=|\text{Table 2}|=-\delta_{3}N_{b+c,a},

N_{-c,b+c}N_{a,-a-b}=|\text{Formulas (002) and (025)}|=2\delta_{1}(\gamma_{1})=2\delta_{1}\gamma_{1},

we have

S=-\delta_{3}N_{b+c,a}+\frac{2\delta_{1}\gamma_{1}}{2}=0

and therefore

[formula]

N_{b+c,a}=\delta_{13}\gamma_{1}.

042. We have
(b+c)+a+(-a-b-c)=0,
therefore (see formula (041))

[formula]

\frac{N_{b+c,a}}{1}=\frac{N_{a,-a-b-c}}{1}=\frac{N_{-a-b-c,b+c}}{2}=\delta_{13}\gamma_{1}.

043. We have
(b+c)+(a+b+c)+(-b)+(-a-b-2c)=0
and
(a+b+c)+(-b)\notin F_{4},
means

S=\frac{N_{b+c,a+b+c}N_{-b,-a-b-2c}}{2}+\frac{N_{-b,b+c}N_{a+b+c,-a-b-2c}}{1}=0.

Since

N_{b+c,a+b+c}N_{-b,-a-b-2c}=|\text{Table 2}|=-\gamma_{2}N_{b+c,a+b+c},

N_{-b,b+c}N_{a+b+c,-a-b-2c}=|\text{Formulas (002) and (022)}|=-\delta_{1}(\delta_{4})=-\delta_{14}.

we have

S=-\frac{\gamma_{2}N_{b+c,a+b+c}}{2}-\delta_{14}=0

and therefore

[formula]

N_{b+c,a+b+c}=-2\delta_{14}\gamma_{2}.

044. We have
(b+c)+(a+b+c)+(-a-2b-2c)=0,
therefore (see formula (043))

[formula]

\frac{N_{b+c,a+b+c}}{2}=\frac{N_{a+b+c,-a-2b-2c}}{1}=\frac{N_{-a-2b-2c,b+c}}{1}=-\delta_{14}\gamma_{2}.

045. We have
b+(a+b+2c+d)+(-d)+(-a-2b-2c)=0
and
(-d)+b\notin F_{4},
means

S=\frac{N_{b,a+b+2c+d}N_{-d,-a-2b-2c}}{1}+\frac{N_{a+b+2c+d,-d}N_{b,-a-2b-2c}}{2}=0.

Since

N_{b,a+b+2c+d}N_{-d,-a-2b-2c}=|\text{Table 2}|=-\epsilon_{8}N_{b,a+b+2c+d},

N_{a+b+2c+d,-d}N_{b,-a-2b-2c}=|\text{Formulas (016) and (026)}|=-2\epsilon_{6}(-\gamma_{2})=2\epsilon_{6}\gamma_{2},

we have

S=-\epsilon_{8}N_{b,a+b+2c+d}+\frac{2\epsilon_{6}\gamma_{2}}{2}=0

and therefore

[formula]

N_{b,a+b+2c+d}=\epsilon_{68}\gamma_{2}.

046. We have
b+(a+b+2c+d)+(-a-2b-2c-d)=0,
therefore (see formula (045))

[formula]

\frac{N_{b,a+b+2c+d}}{1}=\frac{N_{a+b+2c+d,-a-2b-2c-d}}{2}=\frac{N_{-a-2b-2c-d,b}}{1}=\epsilon_{68}\gamma_{2}.

047. We have
(b+c)+(a+2b+3c+2d)+(-b)+(-a-2b-4c-2d)=0
and
(a+2b+3c+2d)+(-b)\notin F_{4},
means

S=\frac{N_{b+c,a+2b+3c+2d}N_{-b,-a-2b-4c-2d}}{2}+\frac{N_{-b,b+c}N_{a+2b+3c+2d,-a-2b-4c-2d}}{1}=0.

Since

N_{b+c,a+2b+3c+2d}N_{-b,-a-2b-4c-2d}=|\text{Table 2}|=-\gamma_{3}N_{b+c,a+2b+3c+2d},

N_{-b,b+c}N_{a+2b+3c+2d,-a-2b-4c-2d}=|\text{Formulas (002) and (024)}|=-\delta_{1}(\delta_{6})=-\delta_{16}.

we have

S=-\frac{\gamma_{3}N_{b+c,a+2b+3c+2d}}{2}-\delta_{16}=0

and therefore

[formula]

N_{b+c,a+2b+3c+2d}=-2\delta_{16}\gamma_{3}.

049. We have

S=\frac{N_{b+2c,a+2b+2c+2d}N_{-b,-a-2b-4c-2d}}{2}+\frac{N_{a+2b+2c+2d,-b}N_{b+2c,-a-2b-4c-2d}}{2}=0.

N_{a+2b+2c+2d,-b}N_{b+2c,-a-2b-4c-2d}=|\text{Formulas (056) and (080)}|=-\epsilon_{6789}\gamma_{2}(-\epsilon_{6780}\delta_{1256}\gamma_{2})=

=\epsilon_{90}\delta_{1256},

we have

S=-\frac{\gamma_{3}N_{b+2c,a+2b+2c+2d}}{2}+\frac{\epsilon_{90}\delta_{1256}}{2}=0

and therefore

[formula]

N_{b+2c,a+2b+2c+2d}=\epsilon_{90}\delta_{1256}\gamma_{3}.

and therefore

[formula]

N_{a+b+2c+d,a+2b+2c+d}=2\epsilon_{60}\delta_{3456}\gamma_{13}\alpha_{1}.

112. We have
(a+b+2c+d)+(a+2b+2c+d)+(-2a-3b-4c-2d)=0,
therefore (see formula (111))

[formula]

\frac{N_{a+b+2c+d,a+2b+2c+d}}{2}=\frac{N_{a+2b+2c+d,-2a-3b-4c-2d}}{1}=\frac{N_{-2a-3b-4c-2d,a+b+2c+d}}{1}=\epsilon_{60}\delta_{3456}\gamma_{13}\alpha_{1}.

113. We have
(a+b+2c+2d)+(a+2b+2c)+(-a)+(-a-3b-4c-2d)=0
and
(a+2b+2c)+(-a)\notin F_{4},
means

S=\frac{N_{a+b+2c+2d,a+2b+2c}N_{-a,-a-3b-4c-2d}}{2}+\frac{N_{-a,a+b+2c+2d}N_{a+2b+2c,-a-3b-4c-2d}}{2}=0.

Since

N_{a+b+2c+2d,a+2b+2c}N_{-a,-a-3b-4c-2d}=|\text{Table 2}|=-\alpha_{1}N_{a+b+2c+2d,a+2b+2c},

N_{-a,a+b+2c+2d}N_{a+2b+2c,-a-3b-4c-2d}=|\text{Formulas (096) and (104)}|=

=(-\epsilon_{3467}\delta_{1234}\gamma_{1})(\epsilon_{3480}\delta_{1256}\gamma_{3})=-\epsilon_{6780}\delta_{3456}\gamma_{13},

we have

S=-\frac{\alpha_{1}N_{a+b+2c+2d,a+2b+2c}}{2}-\frac{\epsilon_{6780}\delta_{3456}\gamma_{13}}{2}=0

and therefore

[formula]

N_{a+b+2c+2d,a+2b+2c}=-\epsilon_{6780}\delta_{3456}\gamma_{13}\alpha_{1}.

114. We have
(a+b+2c+2d)+(a+2b+2c)+(-2a-3b-4c-2d)=0,
therefore (see formula (113))

[formula]

N_{a+b+2c+2d,a+2b+2c}=N_{a+2b+2c,-2a-3b-4c-2d}=N_{-2a-3b-4c-2d,a+b+2c+2d}=-\epsilon_{6780}\delta_{3456}\gamma_{13}\alpha_{1}.

115. We have
c+(a+2b+2c+2d)+(-b-c-d)+(-a-b-2c-d)=0
and
(-b-c-d)+c\notin F_{4},
means

S=\frac{N_{c,a+2b+2c+2d}N_{-b-c-d,-a-b-2c-d}}{1}+\frac{N_{a+2b+2c+2d,-b-c-d}N_{c,-a-b-2c-d}}{1}=0.

Since

N_{c,a+2b+2c+2d}N_{-b-c-d,-a-b-2c-d}=|\text{Formula (069)}|=\epsilon_{2680}\delta_{15}\gamma_{2}N_{c,a+2b+2c+2d},

N_{a+2b+2c+2d,-b-c-d}N_{c,-a-b-2c-d}=|\text{Formulas (062) and (034)}|=\epsilon_{2589}\delta_{14}\gamma_{2}(-\epsilon_{56}\delta_{4})=

=-\epsilon_{2689}\delta_{1}\gamma_{2},

we have

S=\epsilon_{2680}\delta_{15}\gamma_{2}N_{c,a+2b+2c+2d}-\epsilon_{2689}\delta_{1}\gamma_{2}=0,

and therefore

[formula]

N_{c,a+2b+2c+2d}=\epsilon_{90}\delta_{5}.

116. We have
c+(a+2b+2c+2d)+(-a-2b-3c-2d)=0,
therefore (see formula (115))

[formula]

\frac{N_{c,a+2b+2c+2d}}{1}=\frac{N_{a+2b+2c+2d,-a-2b-3c-2d}}{1}=\frac{N_{-a-2b-3c-2d,c}}{2}=\epsilon_{90}\delta_{5}.

To conclude the proof, we present the diagrams that show the dependencies of the formulas.

The theorem is completely proven.
\square

1.3.
Chevalley’s Commutator Formulas

Let
\Phi
be a reduced indecomposable root system,
K
is a
field. According to
[
2
, Theorem 5.2.2]
the commutator

[x_{s}(u),x_{r}(t)]=x_{s}(u)^{-1}x_{r}(t)^{-1}x_{s}(u)x_{r}(t),

where
r,s\in\Phi
and
u,t\in K,
is equal to one if
r+s\notin\Phi
and
r\neq-s,
and expands
into the product of root elements according to the formula

\left[x_{s}(u),x_{r}(t)\right]=\prod_{{\scriptsize\begin{array}[]{c}ir+js\in\Phi,\\
i,j>0\end{array}}}x_{ir+js}(C_{ij,rs}(-t)^{i}u^{j}),

if
r+s\in\Phi.
The product is taken over all pairs of positive integers
i,j
for which
ir+js
is a root, in order of increasing
i+j.
The constant
C_{ij,rs}

are integers and are determined by the formulas
[
2
, theorem 5.2.2]
:

\displaystyle C_{i1,rs}

\displaystyle=M_{r,s,i},

\displaystyle C_{1j,rs}

\displaystyle=(-1)^{j}M_{s,r,j},

\displaystyle C_{32,rs}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{3}M_{r+s,r,2},

\displaystyle C_{23,rs}

\displaystyle=-\frac{2}{3}M_{s+r,s,2}.

In turn, the numbers
M_{r,s,i}
are expressed in terms of structure
constants
N_{r,s}
of the corresponding Lie algebra according to the formula
[
2
, p. 61]

M_{r,s,i}=\frac{1}{i!}N_{r,s}N_{r,r+s}\ldots N_{r,(i-1)r+s},

Let
r,s,r+s\in\Phi.
We have

C_{11,rs}=N_{rs}=-N_{sr}

and

C_{11,-r,-s}=N_{-r,-s}=N_{sr}.

Further, if
2r+s\in\Phi,
then

C_{21,rs}=\frac{1}{2}N_{rs}N_{r,r+s}=-\frac{1}{2}N_{sr}N_{r,r+s}

and

C_{21,-r,-s}=\frac{1}{2}N_{-r,-s}N_{-r,-r-s}=-\frac{1}{2}N_{sr}N_{r,r+s},

and if
r+2s\in\Phi,
then

C_{12,rs}=\frac{1}{2}N_{sr}N_{s,r+s}

and

C_{12,-r,-s}=\frac{1}{2}N_{-s,-r}N_{-s,-r-s}=\frac{1}{2}N_{sr}N_{s,r+s}.

It follows that:

1) if
2r+s,r+2s\notin\Phi,
then

[x_{s}(u),x_{r}(t)]=x_{r+s}(C_{11,rs}(-t)u)=x_{r+s}(N_{sr}tu)

and

[x_{-s}(u),x_{-r}(t)]=x_{-r-s}(C_{11,-r,-s}(-t)u)=x_{r+s}(-N_{sr}tu).

2) if
2r+s\in\Phi,

r+2s\notin\Phi,
then

\begin{array}[]{rcl}[x_{s}(u),x_{r}(t)]&=&x_{r+s}(C_{11,rs}(-t)u)\ x_{2r+s}(C_{21,rs}(-t)^{2}u)=\\[11.38109pt]
&=&x_{r+s}(N_{sr}tu)\ x_{2r+s}(-(1/2)N_{sr}N_{r,r+s}t^{2}u).\\
\end{array}

and

\begin{array}[]{rcl}[x_{-s}(u),x_{-r}(t)]&=&x_{-r-s}(C_{11,-r,-s}(-t)u)\ x_{-2r-s}(C_{21,-r,-s}(-t)^{2}u)=\\[11.38109pt]
&=&x_{-r-s}(-N_{sr}tu)\ x_{-2r-s}(-(1/2)N_{sr}N_{r,r+s}t^{2}u).\\
\end{array}

3) if
2r+s\notin\Phi,

r+2s\in\Phi,
then

\begin{array}[]{rcl}[x_{s}(u),x_{r}(t)]&=&x_{r+s}(C_{11,rs}(-t)u)\ x_{r+2s}(C_{12,rs}(-t)u^{2})=\\[11.38109pt]
&=&x_{r+s}(N_{sr}tu)\ x_{r+2s}(-(1/2)N_{sr}N_{s,r+s}tu^{2}).\\
\end{array}

and

\begin{array}[]{rcl}[x_{-s}(u),x_{-r}(t)]&=&x_{-r-s}(C_{11,-r,-s}(-t)u)\ x_{-r-2s}(C_{12,-r,-s}(-t)u^{2})=\\[11.38109pt]
&=&x_{-r-s}(-N_{sr}tu)\ x_{-r-2s}(-(1/2)N_{sr}N_{s,r+s}tu^{2}).\\
\end{array}

1.4.
List of Formulas

Positive roots

d:

[formula]

[x_{d}(u),x_{c}(t)]=x_{c+d}(N_{d,c}tu)=x_{c+d}(\epsilon_{1}tu).

[formula]

[x_{d}(u),x_{b+c}(t)]=x_{b+c+d}(N_{d,b+c}tu)=x_{b+c+d}(\epsilon_{2}tu).

[formula]

\begin{array}[]{rcl}[x_{d}(u),x_{b+2c}(t)]&=&x_{b+2c+d}(N_{d,b+2c}tu)\ x_{b+2c+2d}(-(1/2)N_{d,b+2c}N_{d,b+2c+d}tu^{2})\\[11.38109pt]
&=&x_{b+2c+d}(\epsilon_{3}tu)x_{b+2c+2d}(-\epsilon_{34}tu^{2}).\end{array}

Explanation:

-(1/2)N_{d,b+2c}N_{d,b+2c+d}=-(1/2)(\epsilon_{3})(2\epsilon_{4})=-\epsilon_{34}.

[formula]

[x_{d}(u),x_{b+2c+d}(t)]=x_{b+2c+2d}(N_{d,b+2c+d}tu)=x_{b+2c+2d}(2\epsilon_{4}tu).

[formula]

[x_{d}(u),x_{a+b+c}(t)]=x_{a+b+c+d}(N_{d,a+b+c}tu)=x_{a+b+c+d}(\epsilon_{5}tu).

[formula]

\begin{array}[]{rcl}[x_{d}(u),x_{a+b+2c}(t)]&=&x_{a+b+2c+d}(N_{d,a+b+2c}tu)\times\\[11.38109pt]
&&x_{a+b+2c+2d}(-(1/2)N_{d,a+b+2c}N_{d,a+b+2c+d}tu^{2})\\[11.38109pt]
&=&x_{a+b+2c+d}(\epsilon_{6}tu)\ x_{a+b+2c+2d}(-\epsilon_{67}tu^{2}).\end{array}

Explanation:

-(1/2)N_{d,a+b+2c}N_{d,a+b+2c+d}=-(1/2)(\epsilon_{6})(2\epsilon_{7})=-\epsilon_{67}.

[formula]

[x_{d}(u),x_{a+b+2c+d}(t)]=x_{a+b+2c+2d}(N_{d,a+b+2c+d}tu)=x_{a+b+2c+2d}(2\epsilon_{7}tu).

[formula]

\begin{array}[]{rcl}[x_{d}(u),x_{a+2b+2c}(t)]&=&x_{a+2b+2c+d}(N_{d,a+2b+2c}tu)\times\\[11.38109pt]
&&x_{a+2b+2c+2d}(-(1/2)N_{d,a+2b+2c}N_{d,a+2b+2c+d}tu^{2})\\[11.38109pt]
&=&x_{a+2b+2c+d}(\epsilon_{8}tu)\ x_{a+2b+2c+2d}(-\epsilon_{89}tu^{2}).\end{array}

Explanation:

-(1/2)N_{d,a+2b+2c}N_{d,a+2b+2c+d}=-(1/2)(\epsilon_{8})(2\epsilon_{9})=-\epsilon_{89}.

[formula]

[x_{d}(u),x_{a+2b+2c+d}(t)]=x_{a+2b+2c+2d}(N_{d,a+2b+2c+d}tu)=x_{a+2b+2c+2d}(2\epsilon_{9}tu).

[formula]

[x_{d}(u),x_{a+2b+3c+d}(t)]=x_{a+2b+3c+2d}(N_{d,a+2b+3c+d}tu)=x_{a+2b+3c+2d}(\epsilon_{0}tu).

c:

[formula]

\begin{array}[]{rcl}[x_{c}(u),x_{b}(t)]&=&x_{b+c}(N_{c,b}tu)\ x_{b+2c}(-(1/2)N_{c,b}N_{c,b+c}tu^{2})\\[11.38109pt]
&=&x_{b+c}(\delta_{1}tu)\ x_{b+2c}(-\delta_{12}tu^{2}).\end{array}

Explanation:

-(1/2)N_{c,b}N_{c,b+c}=-(1/2)(\delta_{1})(2\delta_{2})=-\delta_{12}.

[formula]

[x_{c}(u),x_{b+c}(t)]=x_{b+2c}(N_{c,b+c}tu)=x_{b+2c}(2\delta_{2}tu).

[formula]

[formula]

\begin{array}[]{rcl}[x_{c}(u),x_{a+b}(t)]&=&x_{a+b+c}(N_{c,a+b}tu)\ x_{a+b+2c}(-(1/2)N_{c,a+b}N_{c,a+b+c}tu^{2})\\[11.38109pt]
&=&x_{a+b+c}(\delta_{3}tu)\ x_{a+b+2c}(-\delta_{34}tu^{2}).\end{array}

Explanation:

-(1/2)N_{c,a+b}N_{c,a+b+c}=-(1/2)(\delta_{3})(2\delta_{4})=-\delta_{34}.

[formula]

[x_{c}(u),x_{a+b+c}(t)]=x_{a+b+2c}(N_{c,a+b+c}tu)=x_{a+b+2c}(2\delta_{4}tu).

[formula]

[x_{c}(u),x_{a+b+c+d}(t)]=x_{a+b+2c+d}(N_{c,a+b+c+d}tu)=x_{a+b+2c+d}(\epsilon_{56}\delta_{4}tu).

[formula]

[x_{c}(u),x_{a+2b+2c+d}(t)]=x_{a+2b+3c+d}(N_{c,a+2b+2c+d}tu)=x_{a+2b+3c+d}(\delta_{5}tu).

[formula]

\begin{array}[]{rcl}[x_{c}(u),x_{a+2b+2c+2d}(t)]&=&x_{a+2b+3c+2d}(N_{c,a+2b+2c+2d}tu)\times\\[11.38109pt]

&=&x_{a+2b+3c+2d}(\epsilon_{90}\delta_{5}tu)\ x_{a+2b+4c+2d}(-\epsilon_{90}\delta_{56}tu^{2}).\end{array}

Explanation:

[formula]

[x_{c}(u),x_{a+2b+3c+2d}(t)]=x_{a+2b+4c+2d}(N_{c,a+2b+3c+2d}tu)=x_{a+2b+4c+2d}(2\delta_{6}tu).

[formula]

\begin{array}[]{rcl}[x_{c+d}(u),x_{b}(t)]&=&x_{b+c+d}(N_{c+d,b}tu)\ x_{b+2c+2d}(-(1/2)N_{c+d,b}N_{c+d,b+c+d}tu^{2})\\[11.38109pt]

b+c+d:

[formula]

\begin{array}[]{rcl}[x_{b+c+d}(u),x_{a}(t)]&=&x_{a+b+c+d}(N_{b+c+d,a}tu)\times\\[11.38109pt]

[x_{b+c+d}(u),x_{a+b+c+d}(t)]=x_{a+2b+2c+2d}(N_{b+c+d,a+b+c+d}tu)=x_{a+2b+2c+2d}(-2\epsilon_{2589}\delta_{14}\gamma_{2}tu).

[x_{b+c+d}(u),x_{a+b+c}(t)]=x_{a+2b+2c+d}(N_{b+c+d,a+b+c}tu)=x_{a+2b+2c+d}(-\epsilon_{28}\delta_{14}\gamma_{2}tu).

[formula]

\begin{array}[]{rcl}[x_{b+c+d}(u),x_{a+b+2c}(t)]&=&x_{a+2b+3c+d}(N_{b+c+d,a+b+2c}tu)\times\\[11.38109pt]
&&x_{a+3b+4c+2d}(-(1/2)N_{b+c+d,a+b+2c}N_{b+c+d,a+2b+3c+d}tu^{2})\\[11.38109pt]
&=&x_{a+2b+3c+d}(-\epsilon_{28}\delta_{15}\gamma_{2}tu)\ x_{a+3b+4c+2d}(\epsilon_{80}\delta_{56}\gamma_{23}tu^{2}).\\
\end{array}

Explanation:

-(1/2)N_{a+b+2c+d,-2a-3b-4c-2d}N_{a+b+2c+d,-a-2b-2c-d}=-(1/2)(-\epsilon_{60}\delta_{3456}\gamma_{13}\alpha_{1})(2\epsilon_{68}\gamma_{2})=

=\epsilon_{80}\delta_{3456}\gamma_{123}\alpha_{1}.

[formula]

\begin{array}[]{rcl}[x_{a+b+2c+d}(u),x_{-a-2b-3c-d}(t)]&=&x_{-b-c}(N_{a+b+2c+d,-a-2b-3c-d}tu)\\[11.38109pt]
&=&x_{-b-c}(\epsilon_{68}\delta_{15}\gamma_{2}tu).\end{array}

[formula]

\begin{array}[]{rcl}[x_{a+b+2c+d}(u),x_{-a-2b-3c-2d}(t)]&=&x_{-b-c-d}(N_{a+b+2c+d,-a-2b-3c-2d}tu)\\[11.38109pt]
&=&x_{-b-c-d}(-\epsilon_{2680}\delta_{15}\gamma_{2}tu).\end{array}

[formula]

\begin{array}[]{rcl}[x_{a+b+2c+d}(u),x_{-a-2b-4c-2d}(t)]&=&x_{-b-2c-d}(N_{a+b+2c+d,-a-2b-4c-2d}tu)\\[11.38109pt]
&=&x_{-b-2c-d}(-\epsilon_{3680}\delta_{1256}\gamma_{2}tu).\end{array}

a+b+2c+2d:

[formula]

\begin{array}[]{rcl}[x_{a+b+2c+2d}(u),x_{-a-2b-2c-2d}(t)]&=&x_{-b}(N_{a+b+2c+2d,-a-2b-2c-2d}tu)\\[11.38109pt]
&=&x_{-b}(\epsilon_{6789}\gamma_{2}tu).\end{array}

[formula]

\begin{array}[]{rcl}[x_{a+b+2c+2d}(u),x_{-a-2b-3c-2d}(t)]&=&x_{-b-c}(N_{a+b+2c+2d,-a-2b-3c-2d}tu)\\[11.38109pt]
&=&x_{-b-c}(\epsilon_{6780}\delta_{15}\gamma_{2}tu).\end{array}

[formula]

\begin{array}[]{rcl}[x_{a+b+2c+2d}(u),x_{-a-2b-4c-2d}(t)]&=&x_{-b-2c}(N_{a+b+2c+2d,-a-2b-4c-2d}tu)\\[11.38109pt]
&=&x_{-b-2c}(\epsilon_{6780}\delta_{1256}\gamma_{2}tu).\end{array}

[formula]

\begin{array}[]{rcl}[x_{a+b+2c+2d}(u),x_{-2a-3b-4c-2d}(t)]&=&x_{-a-2b-2c}(N_{a+b+2c+2d,-2a-3b-4c-2d}tu)\\[11.38109pt]
&=&x_{-a-2b-2c}(\epsilon_{6780}\delta_{3456}\gamma_{13}\alpha_{1}tu).\end{array}

a+2b+2c:

[formula]

[x_{a+2b+2c}(u),x_{-a-2b-3c-d}(t)]=x_{-c-d}(N_{a+2b+2c,-a-2b-3c-d}tu)=x_{-c-d}(-\epsilon_{18}\delta_{5}tu).

&=&x_{-b-2c-2d}(\epsilon_{3480}\delta_{1256}\gamma_{3}tu).\end{array}

[formula]

\begin{array}[]{rcl}[x_{a+2b+2c}(u),x_{-2a-3b-4c-2d}(t)]&=&x_{-a-b-2c-2d}(N_{a+2b+2c,-2a-3b-4c-2d}tu)\\[11.38109pt]

List of Formulas

Positive roots

d:

[formula]

[x_{d}(u),x_{c}(t)]=x_{c+d}(tu).

[formula]

[x_{d}(u),x_{b+c}(t)]=x_{b+c+d}(tu).

[formula]

[x_{d}(u),x_{b+2c}(t)]=x_{b+2c+d}(tu)x_{b+2c+2d}(-tu^{2}).

[formula]

[x_{d}(u),x_{b+2c+d}(t)]=x_{b+2c+2d}(2tu).

[formula]

[x_{d}(u),x_{a+b+c}(t)]=x_{a+b+c+d}(tu).

[formula]

[x_{d}(u),x_{a+b+2c}(t)]=x_{a+b+2c+d}(tu)\ x_{a+b+2c+2d}(-tu^{2}).

[formula]

[x_{d}(u),x_{a+b+2c+d}(t)]=x_{a+b+2c+2d}(2tu).

[formula]

[x_{d}(u),x_{a+2b+2c}(t)]=x_{a+2b+2c+d}(tu)\ x_{a+2b+2c+2d}(-tu^{2}).

[formula]

[x_{d}(u),x_{a+2b+2c+d}(t)]=x_{a+2b+2c+2d}(2tu).

[formula]

[x_{d}(u),x_{a+2b+3c+d}(t)]=x_{a+2b+3c+2d}(tu).

c:

[formula]

[x_{c}(u),x_{b}(t)]=x_{b+c}(tu)\ x_{b+2c}(-tu^{2}).

[formula]

[x_{c}(u),x_{b+c}(t)]=x_{b+2c}(2tu).

[formula]

[x_{c}(u),x_{b+c+d}(t)]=x_{b+2c+d}(tu).

[formula]

[x_{c}(u),x_{a+b}(t)]=x_{a+b+c}(tu)\ x_{a+b+2c}(-tu^{2}).

[formula]

[x_{c}(u),x_{a+b+c}(t)]=x_{a+b+2c}(2tu).

[formula]

[x_{c}(u),x_{a+b+c+d}(t)]=x_{a+b+2c+d}(tu).

[formula]

[x_{c}(u),x_{a+2b+2c+d}(t)]=x_{a+2b+3c+d}(tu).

[formula]

[x_{c}(u),x_{a+2b+2c+2d}(t)]=x_{a+2b+3c+2d}(tu)\ x_{a+2b+4c+2d}(-tu^{2}).

[formula]

[x_{c}(u),x_{a+2b+3c+2d}(t)]=x_{a+2b+4c+2d}(2tu).

c+d:

[formula]

[x_{c+d}(u),x_{b}(t)]=x_{b+c+d}(tu)\ x_{b+2c+2d}(-tu^{2}).

[formula]

[formula]

[formula]

Negative roots

[formula]

[x_{a+2b+2c}(u),x_{-a-2b-3c-d}(t)]=x_{-c-d}(-tu).

[formula]

[x_{a+2b+2c}(u),x_{-a-3b-4c-2d}(t)]=x_{-b-2c-2d}(tu).

[formula]

[x_{a+2b+2c}(u),x_{-2a-3b-4c-2d}(t)]=x_{-a-b-2c-2d}(-tu).

a+2b+2c+d:

[formula]

[x_{a+2b+2c+d}(u),x_{-a-2b-2c-2d}(t)]=x_{-d}(tu).

[formula]

[x_{a+2b+2c+d}(u),x_{-a-2b-3c-d}(t)]=x_{-c}(tu).

[formula]

[x_{a+2b+2c+d}(u),x_{-a-2b-3c-2d}(t)]=x_{-c-d}(-tu).

[formula]

[x_{a+2b+2c+d}(u),x_{-a-3b-4c-2d}(t)]=x_{-b-2c-d}(-tu).

[formula]

[x_{a+2b+2c+d}(u),x_{-2a-3b-4c-2d}(t)]=x_{-a-b-2c-d}(tu).

a+2b+2c+2d:

[formula]

[x_{a+2b+2c+2d}(u),x_{-a-2b-3c-2d}(t)]=x_{-c}(tu).

[formula]

[x_{a+2b+2c+2d}(u),x_{-a-3b-4c-2d}(t)]=x_{-b-2c}(tu).

[formula]

[x_{a+2b+2c+2d}(u),x_{-2a-3b-4c-2d}(t)]=x_{-a-b-2c}(-tu).

a+2b+3c+d:

[formula]

[x_{a+2b+3c+d}(u),x_{-a-2b-3c-2d}(t)]=x_{-d}(tu).

[formula]

[x_{a+2b+3c+d}(u),x_{-a-2b-4c-2d}(t)]=x_{-c-d}(-tu).

[formula]

[x_{a+2b+3c+d}(u),x_{-a-3b-4c-2d}(t)]=x_{-b-c-d}(-tu).

[formula]

[x_{a+2b+3c+d}(u),x_{-2a-3b-4c-2d}(t)]=x_{-a-b-c-d}(-tu).

a+2b+3c+2d:

[formula]

[x_{a+2b+3c+2d}(u),x_{-a-2b-4c-2d}(t)]=x_{-c}(tu).

[formula]

[x_{a+2b+3c+2d}(u),x_{-a-3b-4c-2d}(t)]=x_{-b-c}(-tu).

[formula]

[x_{a+2b+3c+2d}(u),x_{-2a-3b-4c-2d}(t)]=x_{-a-b-c}(tu).

a+2b+4c+2d:

[formula]

[x_{a+2b+4c+2d}(u),x_{-a-3b-4c-2d}(t)]=x_{-b}(tu).

[formula]

[x_{a+2b+4c+2d}(u),x_{-2a-3b-4c-2d}(t)]=x_{-a-b}(-tu).

a+3b+4c+2d:

[formula]

[x_{a+3b+4c+2d}(u),x_{-2a-3b-4c-2d}(t)]=x_{-a}(tu).

3.
Numbers
K_{r,s}^{\Delta}
: Calculation and Applications

3.1.
Basic Definitions

In what follows
\Phi
is a reduced indecomposable root system
in the Euclidean space
V
with scalar product

(\,,\,),

\Pi(\Phi)
is its subsystem of fundamental roots;

N_{r,s},

r,s\in\Phi,
are structure constants of the Lie algebra of type
\Phi,

R
an associative-commutative ring with identity,

E\Phi(R)
is an elementary Chevalley group of type
\Phi
over
R
.

Definition 1.

Let
r,s\in\Phi,
and
\emptyset\neq\Delta\subseteq\Pi(\Phi)
.
If there exist
q_{1},\ldots,q_{t}\in\Delta
such that for any integer

i
,
1\leqslant i\leqslant t
, the sum
s+q_{1}+\cdots+q_{i}
is a root,
then the decomposition
r=s+q_{1}+\cdots+q_{t}
will be called an
s
-decomposition of the root
r

with respect to
\Delta
(an
s
-decomposition if
\Delta=\Pi(\Phi)
).

From Definition 1 it follows that if there is an

s
-decomposition of the root
r
with respect to
\Delta
, then
{\rm ht}(r)-{\rm ht}(s)>0

and therefore
r,s\in\Phi^{+}
, or
r,s\in\Phi^{-}
.
Let
r=s+q_{1}+\cdots+q_{t}
and
r=s+q^{\prime}_{1}+\cdots+q^{\prime}_{t^{\prime}}
be two
s
-decompositions of the root
r
with respect to
\Delta.
Since representation of
r
in terms of roots from
\Pi(\Phi)
is unique, we claim that
t=t^{\prime}
and
exists a permutation
\pi
such that
q_{i}=q^{\prime}_{\pi(i)}
,
i=1,\ldots,t
.
In this regard, we say that two
s
-decompositions of
r
with respect to

\Delta
are equal if and only if
\pi
is the identity permutation.

For fixed
r,s\in\Phi
and
\Delta\subseteq\Pi(\Phi)
we define
the set
Q_{r,s}^{\Delta}
.
We put
Q_{s,s}^{\Delta}=\{0\}
and
Q_{r,s}^{\Delta}=\emptyset
if
r\neq s

and there are no
s
-expansions of the root
r
with respect to
\Delta.

Otherwise, the set
Q_{r,s}^{\Delta}
consists of all distinct tuples

(q_{1},\ldots,q_{t})
, where
q_{1},\ldots,q_{t}\in\Delta
, such that the sum

s+q_{1}+\cdots+q_{t}
is an
s
-decomposition of the root
r
with respect to
\Delta
.

Definition 2.

Let
r,s\in\Phi
and
\emptyset\neq\Delta\subseteq\Pi(\Phi)
.
We put
K_{s,s}^{\Delta}=1
and
K_{r,s}^{\Delta}=0
if
Q_{r,s}^{\Delta}=\emptyset.
In the other cases we put

[formula]

K_{r,s}^{\Delta}=\sum_{(q_{1},\ldots,q_{t})\in Q_{r,s}^{\Delta}}N_{s,q_{1}}N_{s+q_{1},q_{2}}\ldots N_{s+q_{1}+\cdots+q_{t-1},q_{t}}.

The concepts listed above, their properties and examples were introduced in
[
3
]
.

On the set of positive roots, we define the directed graph
G(\Phi^{+},\Delta)
as follows.
The vertices of the graph
G(\Phi^{+},\Delta)
are the roots of the set
\Phi^{+}.
Two vertices
s
and
r,
where

s,r\in\Phi^{+},
is connected by an edge directed from
s
to
r
if and only
if there is a fundamental root
q\in\Delta
such that
r=s+q.

Let us denote the adjacency matrix of the graph
G(\Phi^{+},\Delta)
by
T_{+}(\Delta)=(t_{sr})
that is a matrix of order
|\Phi^{+}|\times|\Phi^{+}|
the element
t_{sr}
of which is equal to
1
if there is the edge
(s,r)
in the graph
G(\Phi^{+},\Delta)
and is equal to zero otherwise.
We also define the matrix
N_{+}(\Delta)=(n_{sr})
of order
|\Phi^{+}|\times|\Phi^{+}|
whose element
n_{sr}
is equal to the constant
N_{s,q}
if exists
a simple root
q\in\Delta
such that
r=s+q,
and is equal to zero otherwise. The graph
G(\Phi^{-},\Delta),
matrices
T_{-}(\Delta)
and
N_{-}(\Delta)
are defined in a similar way. The following theorems hold

Theorem 3.

Let
r,s\in\Phi^{+}
(
r,s\in\Phi^{-}
) and
r\neq s
,
\Delta\subseteq\Pi(\Phi).
Then the number of different

s
-decompositions of the root
r
with respect to
\Delta
is equal to the element at the intersection of the row

s
and column
r
of the matrix
(T_{+}(\Delta))^{k}
(respectively
(T_{-}(\Delta))^{k}
), where
k={\rm ht}(r)-{\rm ht}(s).

Theorem 4.

Let
r,s\in\Phi^{+}
(
r,s\in\Phi^{-}
) and
r\neq s,

\Delta\subseteq\Pi(\Phi).
Then the number
K_{r,s}^{\Delta}

is equal to the element at the intersection of row
s
and column
r,
of the matrix
(N_{+}(\Delta))^{k}
(respectively
(N_{-}(\Delta))^{k}
),
where
k={\rm ht}(r)-{\rm ht}(s).

3.2.
Graphs, Matrices, Tables

\Delta=\{a,b,c,d\}.
Below we present the directed weighted graph
G(F_{4}^{-},\Delta),
its adjacency matrix and
matrix of weights, using them we calculate the table of the number of paths and the table of numbers
K_{r,s}^{\Delta}.

for negative

and

Theorem 5.

For any
r,s\in\Phi^{+}
and arbitrary
\Delta\subseteq\Pi(\Phi)
we have the
equality

K_{r,s}^{\Delta}=(-1)^{{\rm ht}(r)-{\rm ht}(s)}\frac{(r,r)}{(s,s)}K_{-s,-r}^{\Delta}.

Table 12.

3.3.
Aplication to Calculate of Commutators in Chevalley Groups

Let us fix the following notation from the theory of commutators. By definition, we assume
[B,\,_{0}A]=B
and

[B,\,_{w}A]=[[B,\,_{w-1}A],A]

for
w\geqslant 1.
The following statements hold
[
3
]
.

Theorem 6.

Let
R
be an associative -commutative ring with identity,
\Phi
be a reduced indecomposable root system,

s_{1},\ldots,s_{l}\in\Phi^{+}
is different roots of height
m,

\Delta=\{r_{1},\ldots,r_{k}\}\subseteq\Pi(\Phi).
Set

A=x_{r_{1}}(1)\ldots x_{r_{k}}(1)
,
B=x_{s_{1}}(t_{1})\ldots x_{s_{l}}(t_{l}),
where
t_{1},\ldots,t_{l}\in R.

Then for any natural number
w
we have

[B,\,_{w}A]=\prod_{{\rm ht}(r)=m+w}x_{r}\left(\sum_{j=1}^{l}t_{j}K_{r,s_{j}}^{\Delta}\right)\ldots\ .

Here the three dots denote the product of root elements corresponding to the root of a height greater than

m+w.

Theorem 7.

Let
R
be an associative -commutative ring with identity,
J=\langle t_{1},\ldots,t_{l}\rangle

\subset R

is a quasiregular ideal, and,
J^{2}=0;
also let
\Phi
be a reduced indecomposable root system,

s_{1},\ldots,s_{l}\in\Phi^{-}
be different roots of height
m,

\Delta=\{r_{1},\ldots,r_{k}\}\subseteq\Pi(\Phi).

Suppose
A=x_{r_{1}}(1)\ldots x_{r_{k}}(1)
,
B=x_{s_{1}}(t_{1})\ldots x_{s_{l}}(t_{l}).

Then for any natural number
w
such that
m+w<0,
we have

[B,\,_{w}A]=\prod_{{\rm ht}(r)=m+w}x_{r}\left(\sum_{j=1}^{l}t_{j}K_{r,s_{j}}^{\Delta}\right)\ldots\ .

Here the three dots denotes the product of diagonal elements and root elements corresponding to the roots
heights greater than
m+w.

Sergey G. Kolesnikov

Siberian Federal University

email: skolesnikov@sfu-kras.ru

Anna I. Polovinkina

Siberian Federal University

email: aiolovinkina@sfu-kras.ru
